Every run held the shared timing lock and was pinned with taskset -c 31. Arm names: base = pristine
(clean, byte-identical to c-base); c-* = clean single-change builds from the pristine tree (see
verify-limiter-2-harness/variants*.py); n-* = truncations; "-st" = stationary dispatch only.
Native lines: [arm] Wn shape cyc/ls med/min rounds [...]. V8 lines: "web" = per-process medians
of rounds, "wmin" = per-process minima, both as cycles per lane-sample isolates.
